- **边界纪律（ADR-0010）**：runtimeVersion 仅用 `readLauncherPackageVersion` 纯 FS 通路（CLI 进程下为 null → stale 判定整体降级 unknown，绝不 spawn）；密钥红线只禁含密钥**配置文件**内容（包元数据 version 字段可读）；布局判定 workspace 声明优先（本机「hoisted 声明 + 仅 lock.yaml 的残留 .pnpm」并存形态实证）；双市场并存（dsh-m+dshmarket 同装）信息级呈现；doctor 永不修复，建议以文字给出。
- **验收结算（2026-10-04 装机实测）**：farmChecked=236（精确命中评审实测值）/ 悬空 0 / 8 空 scope + 12 bak 入清单 / 账实 13:0 / 双市场信息级 / errors=0 → exit=0 / `--json` 可解析。宿主 method 信封正确但 runtimeVersion=null——**发版日新发现**：本机宿主经 pnpm 全局 shim 启动（`node ~/.local/share/pnpm/dsh web`），`readLauncherPackageVersion` 从 shim 三级上溯落空，stale 判定双通路降级（底层事实人工核对成立：农场 dsh 链接 0.1.7-rc.2 vs 运行时 0.2.0-rc.2；版本第三源列下一批）。另录既有 bug：`.bin/dshm` 符号链接静默 no-op（后经考古为 v0.2.0 起潜在、本机 0.9.28 装机形态首次踩中，见 0.9.30 条目——`invokedDirectly` 比较 symlink 路径与 realpath 永假，bin 入口全命令静默 exit 0；修复列 0.9.30 候选，期间用 `node …/dsh-m/lib/cli.js` 直达）。
- **验证**：新增 `tests/doctor.test.mjs`（36 例：布局冲突并存/祖先链反空转/两级 targetVersion/降级路径/零告警清单/023 形态/link 协议/lock peer 后缀/CLI 子进程）+ `tests/doctor-api.test.mjs`（2 例：method 信封真跑 + 空 profile）；全量 1076 pass / 0 fail / 0 skipped。

### 0.9.28 变更：页大小档位去上游化——32/64/96 取代 24/48/96，默认 32

- **动机**：24/48/96 是复刻 dsh-market 筛选面板（0.7.2）时带过来的上游血统数字。甄别后发现 96 早已被 dsh-m 内化为核心参数（`WITH_LATEST_MAX` 探测上限、精选区默认页、fast-open 快照判定基准），真正的上游痕迹只有 24——「砍 24、保 96」即去上游化与兼容性的交集。
- **新档位**：`MARKET_PAGE_SIZES = [32, 64, 96]`（等差 +32，读序顺）、社区区默认 `DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 32`（首页更满，仍低于 0.6.x 历史默认 50 的探测负载，Q46 预算姿态不变）；精选区默认 96 与 core clamp 1..96 不动。
- **联动**：host-api GUI 通道兜底默认 24 → 32（三端一致，不留暗默认）；探测预算注释同步（market.ts / host-api.ts）；README×2 与 DESIGN.md 措辞同步；GLOSSARY「精选区」词条按代码实态锐化（单页无分页 → 常态单页直出 + 超限降级分页）；测试断言与快照 fixture 同步（偏离档位断言改用组内值 64，覆盖「档位内但非默认仍拒写/判假」）。
- **已知一次性影响**：升级后社区区旧快照（limit=24）不再命中 fast-open 判定，首次打开市场多一次正常请求，快照按新默认重建后自愈；不加 legacy 兼容分支。
- **验证**：`npm run build` 成功；全量 1030 pass / 0 fail（8 skipped）。

### 0.9.27 变更：修复遮罩拖选误关——面板内按下、拖出释放不再关面板

- **问题（装机实测 2026-10-03）**：在市场搜索框内左键按下向左拖选（越过面板边界）释放，整个面板被关闭。非浏览器鼠标手势——面板遮罩是裸 `onClick: onClose`，而在面板内容里按下、拖到遮罩上释放时，浏览器把 `click` 派发到按下/释放目标的公共祖先（恰是遮罩），被误判为「点遮罩关面板」。详情 Modal、截图灯箱、兼容确认弹层同属该缺陷类。
- **修复**：新增纯函数 `backdropCloseHandlers`（src/client/backdrop.js）——仅当 **mousedown 与 click 都落在遮罩自身**时才关闭（其余组合一律不关，click 后按位状态复位）；四处遮罩（主面板/详情/灯箱/兼容弹层）统一切换。点遮罩关闭、Esc 关闭、✕ 按钮行为不变。
- **验证**：新增 `tests/client-backdrop.test.mjs`（关闭/两类拖选不关/状态复位/畸形输入安全，5 组断言）；全量 1030 pass / 0 fail。

### 0.9.26 变更：跨区搜索精选稳定前置——摘要行计数与首页所见一致

- **问题（装机实测 2026-10-03）**：搜索「sidebar」摘要行报「⭐ 精选 3 · 社区 315」，首页精选段只见 1 条。非重复计算——`sourceCounts` 不读分类计数（`alsoCategories` 无涉），3 条为真实精选命中；错位根因是摘要行报全局命中数，而相关性排序叠加社区区默认 downloads 降序 tie-break 把弱命中精选压进后页。
- **修复**：`listMarket` 新增 `curatedFirst`——`source='all'` 且 query 非空时精选命中**稳定前置**（稳定分区，分区内相关序不变，社区命中随后）；仅 host-api GUI 通道携带，tools/CLI 不传，搜索排序三端同序不变；单分区/浏览态天然无效。跨页精选段头悬空与计数错位随之消失（精选命中 ≤ 页大小时全数落在首页段内）。
- **验证**：新增测试 ⑮（无 flag 保持交织相关序 / 带 flag 精选前置 / 空 query 与单分区无效）；全量 1025 pass / 0 fail。

### 0.9.25 变更：跨区搜索——浏览分区、搜索全局（社区 + 精选一并命中）

- **跨区搜索**：任一分区（社区/精选）的搜索框升级为全局——`query` 非空时底层查询切 `source=all`，两分区条目统一相关性排序；清空关键词回到本区浏览态。分区制浏览（ADR-0004）与 `dshm_search`/CLI 三端同序不动；服务端唯一增量是 `MarketResult.sourceCounts` 分桶计数。
- **搜索态呈现**：摘要行「⭐ 精选 N · 社区 M」精确计数（`!loading` 门控防旧数字闪现）；精选命中页内置顶分组（段头各自仅在对应段非空时渲染，跨两列 grid）；非社区卡补「精选」徽章；分类 chips 搜索态隐藏、「筛选」按钮随摘要行保留（搜索态只剩页大小组，排序被相关性优先覆盖）；翻页回顶锚点随态切换。
- **修复**：分区 tab 计数改从恒定字段推导（社区 = `acceptedCount - displaced`、精选 = `registryState.count`），不再随查询变化（此前读「最近一次查询的 total」，跨区搜索会污染计数）；搜索提交时清空激活分类（chips 已隐藏，残留分类会成为不可见过滤）。
- **降级**：`sourceCounts` 缺失/畸形时摘要行整体不渲染，列表行为不变；旧快照/旧宿主响应形状漂移免疫。

### 0.9.24 变更：排除条目代管——供应链等待期从「提前拒绝」到「治理 + 登记」（ADR-0009）

- **问题（实机 2026-10-03 实证）**：0.9.19 的委派前预检把「目标版本未满 24h 等待期」预测成"官方管理器必拦"并提前拒绝——同目标 dsh-market 却能装上（copilot-auth@1.2.4 发布 8 分钟、quota-watch@0.1.21 两例）。pnpm 11.7 默认非严格策略对显式点名的新版本本来就放行并自动登记排除条目，预检的墙并不存在。
- **三挂点代管**：① 委派前治理——desktop profile 排除块的坏形态（pnpm 自追加的死规则）合并为"每包一条、版本并集复合"；② 装机成功后登记——等待期内目标并入排除块（scoped 精确单条 / 非 scoped 目标+上一版双选择器）；③ 双码失败（`ERR_PNPM_MINIMUM_RELEASE_AGE_VIOLATION` / `ERR_PNPM_NO_MATURE_MATCHING_VERSION`）→ 治理 → 至多重试一次，仍败走既有失败翻译与账实分裂复读。web ladder 同套三挂点。
- **边界与安全**：红线收窄出唯一例外（desktop 仅此块、仅委派前后挂点、原子写、解析失败即弃、留痕不落全文）；治理/登记全程持官方同款锁并 fail-open——任何失败不阻塞委派，pnpm 仍是最终执行者；显式设置 age 或 strict 的 profile 仍前置拒绝并给可重试时刻；机理按"首条规则生效"口径改判（know-how 020 §2.4 形态论退役）。
- **文档**：决策全文 `docs/adr/0009-release-age-exclude-governance.md`（含 9 项评估过不做的栅栏附录与观察预案）；GLOSSARY 新词「首条规则/排除条目/治理/登记/等待期」。

### 0.9.23 变更：已装页两段加载——列表秒开 + 更新提示就地补 ⬆（ADR-0008）

- **问题（实机 2026-10-03 实证）**：更新探测焊在已装列表接口里且带 60min 内存 TTL——发版后重开面板吃到陈旧缓存，升级提示迟迟不出现（quota-watch 新版发布 17 分钟后面板仍无提示，dshmarket 同刻已见）。
- **两段并行（零新增按钮）**：第一段 `installed {probe:false}` 立即返回已装列表（registry 匹配与开关相位不受影响）；第二段新方法 `installedUpdates`（`probeMode: 'only'`，**TTL=0 每次挂载真实探测**）完成后把 ⬆ 徽标 / tab 红点 / 「全部升级 (N)」就地补上；探测失败仍逐项走「检查未完成」，不冒充「没得更新」。
- **边界与副作用（ADR-0008 如实留痕）**：范围仅已装页——市场浏览页探测、`dshm_list`/`dshm_outdated` 工具、CLI 行为不变（缺省 `probeMode: 'full'`）；ttl=0 会先删 host namespace 共享缓存条目再重探，浏览页/工具的 TTL 内命中被刷新为更新值（数据只更新鲜、代码路径与自身 TTL 制度不变），GitHub 被动预算消耗速率上升（上限 25/req、50/h 滚动不变），超限走 latestError 优雅降级。
- **文档**：决策全文 `docs/adr/0008-installed-two-phase-probe.md`；DESIGN「已装页/缓存语义」补句；GLOSSARY 新词「两段加载」。

### 0.9.22 变更：升级生效判定——tarball 差异三态分类 + 三端重启提示分流（ADR-0007）

- **问题（0.9.21 实证）**：`@iasiv5/dsh-skins` 1.2.3→1.3.0 升级后未重启即已生效（客户端 bundle rev 热更），三端仍无条件提示「需要重启」——警报疲劳会侵蚀提示的权威性，重启本身也有真实成本（web 服务瞬断 / desktop 手动重开）。
- **生效判定（GLOSSARY「生效判定/纯客户端更新」）**：npm 源升级成功点拉取新旧两版 tarball（并行、总 deadline 10s、单包 8MiB、无缓存），零依赖 ustar 只读解析（支持 pax 长名）+ 逐文件 sha256 diff，分类 `activation: 'client-only' | 'restart-required' | 'unknown'`。规则五条：client 集合 = `exports['./client']` 目标；`dsh.bundle.patch` 声明的补丁目标变更 → 宿主；`package.json` 忽略顶层 version 后语义比较（dependencies 等字段变化照常算宿主）；其余差异按路径归属；client 指向变化保守判宿主。**fail-open**：一切异常 → unknown → 现状提示，绝不影响升级成功态。
- **三端分流**：agent 工具消息 client-only 明示「刷新页面即可生效，不要询问 dshm_restart」、unknown 保守建议重启；GUI toast 后缀 + `needsRestart` 门（client-only 不亮重启横幅；纯函数 `upgradeNotify` 可单测）；CLI 行文案换挡。`needsRestart` 源头放宽 boolean（TS2430 规避），`UpgradeResult`/desktop 升级结果新增 `activation` 字段；接线点 `upgradePluginLocked` + `desktopUpgradeLocked`，selfUpgrade/install/uninstall/github 源维持现状。
- **测试与文档**：新增 ustar 解析 11 例、分类规则+fail-open 18 例、升级接线 7 例（npm/github/selfUpgrade/desktop）、renderUpgrade 4 例、upgradeNotify 3 例；既有升级替身统一补 `classifyActivation` 缝（防隐式出网）。决策与已知局限全文见 `docs/adr/0007-activation-classification.md`（client chunk require 图不追踪；docs 类随版差异保守判宿主侧）。

### 0.9.21 变更：设置页「强制刷新」显示连带修复 + registry.json 变更自动清 jsDelivr 缓存

- **设置页强刷显示修复（实机 2026-10-03 实证）**：设置页「强制刷新」只重载 `registry` 接口（force 同步强刷全链路），但面板展示的 registryState **优先读 `registry-config` 的挂载时快照**——于是出现「toast 报已强制刷新、生效来源/更新时间/条目数纹丝不动」的假死，重开设置页才对齐。现在强刷成功后连带重载 `registry-config`（force 已更新 controller 内存快照，零成本取新值），显示即时跟随生效数据。
- **jsDelivr 自动清缓存（`registry.yml` 新增 `purge-jsdelivr` job）**：默认链「线路粘性 + CDN 恒 200 即成功」会让 jsDelivr 的滞后快照**无限钉死**精选清单——实证：0.9.18 收录 DSH Market（18→19）后，粘性在 jsDelivr 的本机持续拉回 18 条旧版，raw 主线路永远轮不到，用户侧强制刷新也无解（唯有手动 `purge.jsdelivr.net`）。现在 push 到 main 且 `registry.json` 实际变更时（`github.event.before` diff 判定），`validate` 全绿后自动调 purge 接口清缓存并回读 cdn 验证条目数，结果写入 step summary；registry.json 未变的 push 与 PR 全部跳过。

### 0.9.20 变更：latest 探测缓存退回纯内存（重启即失效）+ mutation 定向失效（ADR-0006）

- **事故复盘落地（2026-10-03 凌晨）**：0.9.14 起 latest 探测缓存 write-through 落盘、跨重启存活，失效通道只有 TTL 一条——00:22:58 缓存写入后，00:27–01:05 连发四版、01:07 重启 DSH、01:13 重开面板全部吃到陈旧值，卡片与 `dshm_outdated` 双双误报「已是最新」。本次推翻该设计，决策与取舍全文见 `docs/adr/0006-latest-cache-memory-only.md`。
- **缓存退回纯内存**：latest 探测结果只存内存 Map + TTL（`cacheTtlMin`，默认 60 分钟），**重启即失效**——「发完版重启一下就能看到」重新成立。代价是重启后首轮受限重探（当前页条目、8 并发 + deadline 兜底，TTL 内只付一次）；registry/社区**目录正文**的落盘缓存与 SWR 不受影响，页面骨架照旧秒开。0.9.14 遗留的 `<cacheRoot>/latest/<ns>.json` 惰性文件由探测段一次性 best-effort 清扫。
- **mutation 定向失效**：install/upgrade/uninstall 事务**成功点**按 itemId 作废该条目全部 registryKey 缓存变体（浏览页键 / 已装页 matched 键 / npm-only 键）——升级后卡片不再出现「已装新版 / 最新旧版」自相矛盾；`dshm_outdated` 对刚升级包诚实。只失效不回写（故意装旧版时回写会伪造 latest=已装）；事务回滚路径零失效；卸载对 github 源条目的 `gh:` 键尽力而为（pkg 名不可逆推 repo，交由 TTL 自然过期）。
- **不做项留痕**：force 穿透探测缓存与浏览页 GitHub 预算对齐经主人拍板暂缓——前者残余盲区仅「不重启 + TTL 内」窗口，后者对现行全 npm 源精选清单收益为零（ADR-0006 §Considered Options）。

### 0.9.19 变更：供应链等待期「锁文件校验」实证修复——委派前预检 + 全量违规解析 + 账实分裂明示

- **根因（本机 2026-10-03 00:15 实证）**：pnpm 11.7 对 desktop profile 做**锁文件级**供应链校验（`Verifying lockfile against supply-chain policies (180 entries)`），而它给每次成功安装自动追加的 `minimumReleaseAgeExclude` **非 scoped 独立精确条目不被这次校验认可**（`dsh-m@0.9.18` 即被拒；scoped 的 `'@iasiv5/dsh-quota-watch@0.1.13'` 则认可）——装过一个「太新」版本后，**等待期内任何官方包操作都会被这个旁包条目拦死**，与本次目标无关；且校验失败前目标包已被写入 node_modules、官方管理器只回滚 manifest/lockfile → 界面显示新版 active、操作记录却是失败（**「账实分裂」**），下次包操作还会把插件静默回退。
- **委派前预检（`releaseAgePrecheck`，只读、零文件级红线不破、fail-open）**：npm 源安装/升级在委派官方管理器前，对目标版本与锁内「不被校验认可的独立精确排除条目」逐个核对 registry 发布时刻，任一未满等待期 → 结构化 `release-age-wait` 拒绝（含各自可重试时刻），不再产生半写状态；发布时刻不可得 / 策略不可读 / 命中有效排除条目（包名级、`||` 复合、scoped 独立精确）→ 放行，pnpm 仍是最终执行者。
- **失败翻译换新（替代 0.9.10 单条解析）**：解析全部违规条目，按「本次目标 vs 锁内旁包」分述发布时刻与可重试时刻——旁包连坐不再被冒充成目标被拦；移除「改用 DSH Web 安装」的失配指引。`DesktopOpsError` 新增结构化 `details`（violations/targetViolating/splitState/blockers）。
- **账实分裂复读**：等待期失败后复读实装状态，node_modules 已是目标版本而 manifest 仍旧版时，在错误信息中明示「下次包操作会回退到旧版，等待期满重新升级即可对齐」。
- **接线**：desktop install / self-upgrade 调用点补穿 `profileDir`（升级路径此前已有）。

### 0.9.18 变更：精选收录 DSH Market + README 重构与变更日志外迁

- **新收录 DSH Market**（npm `dshmarket`，精选 18→19）：三方可视化插件市场——浏览、搜索社区插件并一键安装，主题一键热切换；主桶装机必备、次桶崔添翼精选（`alsoCategories` 次级归属）。文案按收录规范三句式，三方条目不设 `verified`。
- **文档面重构**：README/README.en 重写为精简结构（亮点、TOC、环境要求、文档索引、支持矩阵 FAQ），推荐安装路径改为 DSH 官方插件管理界面（附截图与升级限制提示）；本变更日志自 README 外迁（双语维护）；Desktop FAQ 对齐 0.9.8 后能力面；DESIGN §3/§12 补实机核验与实测代际记录。
- **附带**：registry 守卫测试条数 18→19；`package.json` files 新增 CHANGELOG.md 随包发布。

### 0.9.17 变更：策展桶次级归属（一插件跨桶）+「iasi自研」更名

- **次级归属 `alsoCategories`**：收录条目可声明次级策展桶——chips 计数与桶过滤按「主桶 ∪ 次桶」计（跨桶条目在每个桶里都出现），详情页分类标签仍随主桶。首批双席位：**better-sidebar**（装机必备 ⊕ 崔添翼精选）、**dsh-m**（装机必备 ⊕ iasi自研）；better-sidebar 原第 5 个「崔添翼精选」tag 由真实席位取代（tags 回归 ≤4 软规范）。
- **更名**：策展桶「我的自研」→「**iasi自研**」（slug `self-dev` 不变，chips/工具/CLI/详情标签四端同步）。
- **例行过渡**：schema 加字段后旧客户端校验拒收新 registry → 回落缓存/包内快照显示旧数据，升级即愈。

### 0.9.16 变更：精选策展五桶分类法 + 严筛收录（23→19）+ 新收录 DSH TUI

- **策展分类法**：精选区分类从功能五分类（市场/工具/界面/搜索/其他）改为**策展五桶**——装机必备 / 崔添翼精选 / iasi自研 / 腾讯轻量云专区 / 观察区（chips 按此序，腾讯轻量云垫后）。分类语义从「插件是什么」转为「为什么值得进精选」；重叠归属按 装机必备 > 崔添翼精选 > 自研 > 腾讯轻量云 优先级归桶，功能属性转 tags 保留检索，旧分类值仍可作开放 slug 命中自定义源（ADR-0004 修订）。
- **严筛收录 23→19**：通道五件套（lark/qqbot/weixin/wecom/dingtalk）退出精选——社区层（4,000+ 条）仍收录可装，只是退出策展位；新增收录 **DSH TUI**（崔添翼 9/26 X 推荐：终端 TUI 客户端，`dsh-tui`，走 DSH 客户端契约 ctx.remote）；better-sidebar 因优先级归装机必备，以 tag「崔添翼精选」保留 9/27 推荐出处。
- **同版本携带**（本地增强随发版收编）：desktop 安装 enable 阶段失败自动重试一次（高频装卸/插件树重载竞速实证）+ 失败文案按 packageResult 精确化；市场页「缓存快照」横幅退役——stale 状态由设置页社区卡承接，浏览页不再提示临时缓存状态。

### 0.9.15 变更：安装进度/终态就地进详情 Modal

- **场景**：从详情 Modal 点「安装」后，安装信息（pnpm 阶段进度行、「变更完成」横幅/toast）都渲染在面板底层，隔着 Modal 遮罩半透明透出——弹窗内只有按钮转圈，看不出装到哪一步（实机截图反馈 2026-10-02）。卸载无此问题：不弹 Modal，状态本来就挂在已装卡片上。
- **变更**：安装进行中，Modal 内直接挂进度行（复用 host status 轮询：阶段/进度条/当前包）；终态在 Modal 内就地显示结果行——成功附版本与构建脚本说明 + 重启提示（desktop 给官方应用生命周期指引），失败/守卫拦截附原因且按钮回到可重试，「已跳过」中性呈现；Modal 打开时底层同源进度行让位（关闭 Modal 后照常回归）。信息全部派生自全局操作记录与安装结果，「状态不挂卡片」的所有权模型不变（DESIGN §2.6）。

### 0.9.14 变更（市场秒开四件套 + 详情 Modal 安装命令显隐）

- **市场秒开四件套**：针对「每次打开市场必现『加载收录清单中…』」的加速组合拳——
  - **SWR 先回缓存**：精选清单与社区目录的 TTL 过期不再同步等网络——磁盘缓存存在即**立即返回快照**（社区侧照常显示「缓存快照」横幅，绝不冒充最新），后台单飞自愈，下次打开即新；「强制刷新」按钮语义不变（始终同步强刷，社区卡不连坐）。
  - **线路粘性**：默认双线路（GitHub 原始文件 → jsDelivr 镜像）按缓存记录的**上次成功线路**排序——镜像成功过就先走镜像，不再每次白等主线路失败（大陆网络实测每次冷打开省 10-20s 等待）。
  - **探测缓存落盘**：页条目 npm/GitHub 版本探测缓存从纯内存改为磁盘信封（`latest/`，跨重启存活）——DSH 服务重启后首次打开市场不再重放探测。
  - **客户端快照**：默认首页响应存浏览器本地（10 分钟 TTL），打开面板先渲染上次数据再后台换新——加载 spinner 仅首次使用（无任何快照）出现。
- **详情 Modal「安装命令」折叠行按上下文显隐**：
  - **场景**：折叠行命令推导写死 `dsh plugin --profile web add …`，社区条目的上游 install 原文也同为 --profile web 语义——两个来源都不看当前宿主 profile。desktop 上下文里照抄会把包装进 web profile（当前界面看不见）；已安装条目还挂着命令纯属噪音（实机截图实证：desktop + dsh-m 自身条目，`已安装` 徽章与命令同屏）。
  - **变更**：desktop 上下文与已安装条目**整行隐藏**；web 未安装条目行为不变（CLI bootstrap 路径保留，「30 秒上手」同款命令）。desktop 装机正路是弹窗内「安装」按钮（官方 pluginManager 委派，ADR 0005 纪律）。不做「精简命令去掉 --profile」——显式 `--profile web` 是 0.9.0 拍板的设计（README「profile 目标」节），裸命令的默认 profile 语义含糊，精简反而更差。

### 0.9.13 变更：desktop 角标悬停文案更新（主人拍板）

- 旧文案「当前 DSH profile：{name}（Desktop 首发仅支持只读市场、安装新包与开关）」在 0.9.8 开放升级/卸载/自升级后已过时；按主人拍板改为「**当前生效 Profile**」（en: Active profile）。

### 0.9.12 变更：0.9.11 的重发

0.9.11 在 npmjs 遭遇幽灵发布：OIDC 发布被受理并 **staged**（CLI exit 0、provenance 已上 Transparency Log），但从未 commit 进注册表——GET 404、同版本重发 409 `Cannot publish over previously staged version`。等待自愈无果后按标准解法换号重发。**内容与 0.9.11 完全一致：设置页「强制刷新」不再连坐社区清单卡。**

### 0.9.11 修复：设置页「强制刷新」不再连坐社区清单卡（force 语义只属精选链）

- **场景**：弱网下点精选清单的「强制刷新」，社区清单卡跟着变「不可用 + 获取超时」——根因是 host 把 `force` 一路透传给社区目录 summary，强制重开获取 flight；弱网下 flight 3s 完不成，waiter 超时返回占位摘要（flight 本身在后台 30s hard cap 内继续，跑完即自愈）。
- **修复**：`registry` 的 force 不再透传社区 summary——「强制刷新」语义只属精选链（registry 链）；社区目录走自己的 TTL/共享 flight（首次打开等边界场景在极差网络下仍可能瞬时超时，但强刷不再触发）。

### 0.9.10 修复：Desktop 自升级撞上官方供应链等待期（minimumReleaseAge）→ 诚实指引发成可读

- **场景**：点升级角标走官方管理器时，desktop profile 的 pnpm 供应链策略（`minimumReleaseAge`，发布满 24h 才可安装）拒绝了刚发布的版本——`ERR_PNPM_MINIMUM_RELEASE_AGE_VIOLATION`。**这是策略在正确工作**（防供应链攻击的发布等待期），不是故障；但 dsh-m 此前把它当普通失败甩一屏 pnpm 原文。
- **修复**：管理器结果判定识别该策略码，翻译成诚实指引——点名等待期内的条目与发布时刻、按策略推算「预计何时可重试」，并给出等待期内的替代路径（DSH Web 安装同版本）。绝不做策略绕过（等待期是防供应链攻击的红线，dsh-market #732 同态度）。
- 附带：community 获取链 ⑨ 的挂起上限断言 2s→10s（全量套件并行定时器饥饿下两次闪断；契约「不永久挂起」不变）。

### 0.9.9 修复：备用线路接住后不再弹红色「主线路失败」提示（提示收敛）

- **场景**：默认精选清单双线路（raw → jsDelivr）里主线路 `default-raw` 失败、备用线路成功接管时，设置页仍弹红色「远端提示：default-raw 失败：fetch failed…可稍后重试或检查网络后重试」——对着已经自愈的数据报警，还带着不成立的建议（大陆网络下 raw 间歇不可达是常态，这正是备用线路存在的原因）。
- **修复**：后续线路成功 = 先行失败已自愈，`errors` 不再携带（设置页「生效来源」行已如实标注当前线路，如「GitHub 镜像（备用）」）；全线路失败落 cache/bundled 时错误照常保留——那才是需要行动的信号。custom 链（自定义源失败回退缓存）行为不变，仍然提示。
- 顺带补了 default 链的测试缝（`defaultRoutes` 覆写），双线路收敛与全挂保留各有回归门。

### 0.9.8 修复：Desktop 包操作全面接通官方管理器（安装恒 no-manager；升级/卸载/自升级开放）

- **安装恒失败的根因**：Host API 的 desktop 安装分支调 `desktopInstall(id, cfg, opts)` 漏传第 4 参 deps——`getService` 根本没进适配器，desktop 安装恒报「官方 pluginManager 服务不可用（fail-closed）」（100% 必现，与时机无关）。工具面 `dshm_install` 同病。
- **服务解析与 dsh-market 同源**（借鉴其 official-desktop 接线，本机两轮覆盖安装实证）：探测改双上下文（webServer 注入回调的 hostCtx 优先）+ cordis inject 惰性拉起兜底（短超时）——官方 pluginManager 是惰性服务，未被拉起前一次性 get 恒 undefined；仍缺席才结构化拒绝（绝不文件级回退的红线不动）。
- **能力表扩充（主人裁决，借鉴 dsh-market 策略）**：desktop 的 upgrade（installBundle 覆盖安装）/ uninstall（removeBundle）/ self-upgrade（installBundle('dsh-m@latest')）全部开放——dshmarket 正是这样完成 dsh-m 0.9.3→0.9.4/0.9.5 两轮升级的；判定纪律沿用（application/stage 为准、overridden 非失败、build-blocked 结构化回传 pendingBuilds、listBundles 复读不冒充成功）。restart 继续拒绝（Electron 生命周期归官方壳）。
- GUI 与工具面（dshm_install / dshm_uninstall / dshm_upgrade）三入口同批接线。

### 0.9.7 修复：Desktop 下点升级角标弹红色「升级失败」（能力表拒绝应为指导而非报错）

- **根因**：0.9.1 的升级角标点击后一律调 `self-upgrade`；Desktop 能力表按设计 409 结构化拒绝，但客户端把 409 当普通失败渲染成红色「升级失败」横幅——按能力表这根本不是失败，是「该走官方入口」的指引。
- **修复**：`api()` 透传能力表拒绝的结构化字段（code/action/profile/guidance），角标点击收到 409 时改出**中性 info 横幅**展示官方入口指引（guidance 单一事实源仍在服务端 `active-profile.ts`，客户端零复制）；info 横幅停留 12s。Web 端真实失败仍走红色 err 横幅，行为不变。

### 0.9.6 修复：「清除已完成」对失败记录无声 no-op

- **根因**：0.7.0 评审共识把「失败/已跳过」排除在清除范围外（保留供回看），于是按钮对着一条失败记录点击毫无反应、也无任何反馈——Windows 实机被当成 bug 上报（2026-10-01）。显式点击清除不是「静默抹掉」，旧共识被推翻。
- **修复**：「清除已结束」（原「清除已完成」）现在清除**全部终态**（done/warned/failed/superseded），在途态（queued/running/input）不受影响；没有可清终态时按钮置灰并带说明 tooltip，不再无声 no-op。单条 ✕ 照旧逐条删除。
- **英文文案**同步改为 "Clear ended"。

### 0.9.5 修复：Desktop 下 GUI 读路径漏接 active profile（已装页恒显 web）

- **根因**：0.9.0 双 profile 接线时，Host API 的 `installed` / `market` 两个读路径漏传 active profile——`listInstalledWithMeta` / `listMarket` 内部落回 `webProfileDir()`：Desktop 下已装页恒显「web profile 尚未安装任何插件」、市场「已安装」徽标恒空（agent 工具面 `dshm_list` 同链路已接线，故只有 GUI 错）。
- **修复**：两处补齐 `profileDir: profile.dir` + `profile: profile.name`（与 tools 面同款，profileContext 单一事实源）；registry/社区缓存随 `profile` 参数自动落到 desktop 段。
- **文案**：已装空态/加载态与 profile 提示里的「web profile」硬编码改为 profile 中性表述（实际路径照旧展示）。

### 0.9.4 修复：Windows Desktop 全屏后头部不可点（tab / 还原键被系统标题栏吞掉）

- **根因**：Desktop（Windows）以 `titleBarStyle:hidden + titleBarOverlay` 运行——窗口顶部 40px 是壳的全宽 `-webkit-app-region:drag` 拖拽带（按布局参与拖拽判定、无视 z-index 与绘制顺序），右上角另有系统绘制的 — □ ✕ 悬浮于一切内容之上。面板全屏后头部（tab、还原/关闭键）正好落进这条带：点击被窗口拖拽吞掉、还原键被系统键遮挡，全屏无法退出。
- **修复**：与壳自家 overlay 同款对策——消费壳在 `html` 上设的 `--dsh-windows-titlebar-height` 让出该带（全屏态 `top:var(…)`；浮动态 `padding-top:max(24px,var(…))` 顺带修掉矮窗口下浮板顶边被带压住的边缘情况）。
- **Web 零漂移**：DSH Web / 浏览器无此变量，回落 0px，行为与 0.9.3 完全一致。

### 0.9.3 修复：Windows 原生适配（Windows 宿主实机回归）

- **registry 原子写**：临时文件名此前用 `split('/')` 从绝对路径取尾段，而 Windows 路径分隔符是 `\`——取到的是整条路径（内嵌 `\` 即目录分隔符），临时文件 open 必败且被吞，cache 与 accepted metadata 写入**静默全失败**；改用 `basename()`，三平台一致。
- **本地文件清单地址解析**：`C:\…` 盘符与 `\\server\share` UNC 此前被误当 URL scheme（报「只允许 HTTPS」的误导性错误），`file://C:/…` 会归一成无盘符悬空路径必 ENOENT；现 POSIX 绝对 / Windows 盘符 / UNC 三形态均正确解析为 file kind。
- **构建**：`node_modules/.bin/tsc` 是 POSIX sh shim，Windows 下 `spawnSync ENOENT`；改用当前 Node 直跑 TypeScript 的 JS 入口，`npm run build` 三平台一致。
- **测试面**：Windows 实机全量 829 用例 41 败 → **0**（821 pass / 8 skipped）；POSIX 进程组/SIGTERM 时序、symlink 权限、平台路径断言、固定 sleep 时序假设逐项标注 skip 或平台无关化/有界轮询，测试契约不变。

### 0.9.2 修复：跨服务重启后头部角标停留旧版本

- **一键重启确认新进程后就地刷新**：boot id 确认 DSH Web 已恢复的瞬间，面板立即重取 ping——`dsh-m vX.Y.Z` 角标与 profile chip 同步到新进程数据，不再停留旧版本（0.9.1 实测：芯片升级 + 重启后仍显 v0.9.0）。
- **页面回前台时重取**：面板常开、服务在后台被外部重启的场景，`visibilitychange` 回前台即重取 ping（零轮询成本）。
- **角标版本护栏**：self-check 判定携带的版本与当前进程不符（旧进程残留判定）时一律静默，杜绝「v0.9.1 ⬆ v0.9.1」式误渲染。

### 0.9.1 新增：头部版本角标升级提示（有更新才点亮）

- **静默口径**：面板头部 `dsh-m vX.Y.Z` 角标常态维持 0.7.5 起的静态展示——已是最新、检查失败、本地 dev 版领先 npm（ahead）一律不打扰（ahead 仅在悬停 title 里提示「本地开发版」）。
- **仅 outdated 点亮**：`self-check`（npm latest vs 装机版本，只读）判定有新版本时，角标点亮为 warn 态并显示 `⬆ v<最新>`；点击即触发 `self-upgrade`（同一 mutation session + 装后守卫），成功后出「⚡ 一键重启」横幅。Desktop 下点击按能力表结构化拒绝（409，带官方生命周期指引）。
- **TTL 缓存 + 版本护栏**：检查结果在浏览器 localStorage 缓存 30 分钟，开面板不重复打 npm registry；升级重启后缓存按版本号自动作废。检查失败静默，不弹任何错误。

### 0.9.0 新增：官方 Desktop（双 profile）支持

- **同一包、两个 profile**：dsh-m 现在可装入官方 Desktop 的 `desktop` profile（`~/.dsh/profiles/desktop`），与 Web 的 `web` profile 并列；市场目录、面板与 agent 工具同一套，管理对象始终是宿主当前 profile（官方 `profileContext` 单一事实源）。
- **入口信任检查改委派官方**：`/dshm` 全部 method（含 ping 与未知 method）在读取请求体之前委派官方 `connection.requestRejection()` 判定（trustedHosts / loopback / 跨站 / `Origin: null` 语义随宿主），被拒请求零 body 消耗、零业务调用；宿主缺该能力时 fail-closed 全拒。**行为变化**：旧版自制守卫「缺 Origin 一律 403」不再存在——Desktop 桥合法剥除 Origin 的请求按官方语义放行，无凭据的健康检查探针从「一律 403」变为「按宿主信任判定」。
- **Desktop 首发能力表**：只读市场 + **安装新包**（委派官方 `pluginManager.installBundle`，完整性/锁/生效相位归官方）+ **开关**（委派官方管理器，服务缺席结构化拒绝、绝不文件级 fallback）；**升级 / 卸载 / dsh-m 自更新 / 一键重启**在 Desktop 结构化拒绝（409，带官方入口指引——官方暂无 upgrade API，重启归 Electron 生命周期）；构建脚本按官方 `pendingBuilds` 名单精确重试，绝不全量放行。
- **读模型与缓存按 profile 隔离**：市场安装标注、已装列表、README 预览只读当前 profile；registry / 社区清单 / accepted-source 缓存按 profile 分段（web 沿用旧路径，零迁移零清空）；收藏与操作记录按浏览器 origin 各自独立，Web 与 Desktop 不自动同步。
- **CLI 恒作用于 web profile**：`--profile web` 显式声明；`--profile desktop` 明确拒绝并指引官方 Desktop 插件管理页。
- **如实声明**：Desktop 实机（Win/macOS）E2E 未跑，`registry.json` verified 数组**不新增** Desktop 代际（实测后按收录纪律补录）；Desktop 下不做 dsh-m 文件级装后守卫（app.asar 打包布局探测盲区），以官方结果判定 + `listBundles` 复读替代。

### 0.8.5 修复

- **dshm_upgrade 守卫拦截假成功**：升级命中装后守卫拦截时（如 link/file 来源插件无法自动回退），文本输出误渲染为「✅ undefined 已升级（最新）」；现如实输出拦截原因、补偿终态与修复依据，与卡片标题（守卫拦截）一致。

### 0.8.4 变更：分类标签随界面语言双语化

- **社区分类**：已知 23 个分类的英文名直接取上游目录 `categories.en`，英文界面下分类 chips 与详情/收藏 Modal 的分类行显示英文；缺英文名的上游新分类回退中文，仍按原样渲染进临时组，等发版收录。中文界面不变。
- **精选分类**：五个分类 chip 改走双语字典（市场/工具/界面/搜索/其他 ⇄ Market/Tools/UI/Search/Other），与详情 Modal 口径一致。
- **实现**：summary 新增 `categoryLabelsEn`（`communityOutcome` 从上游目录派生，缺 en 的 id 不进映射，不手养第二张表），客户端按界面语言合并取值。0.8.1–0.8.3 为本地迭代号，无独立变更面，不单列。

### 0.8.0 变更：设置页重做（对齐双清单分区定位）

- **信息架构**：社区清单（from awesome-dsh-plugin）→ 精选清单（registry.json）→ dsh-m 自身 → 关于；「主清单」的 UI 可见名统一为「精选清单」。
- **社区目录开关**：新增 GUI 开关（live 生效，即时切换无确认；关闭态整卡收为一行说明）；新增 `set-community` API。
- **精选清单瘦身**：状态字段 7 行收敛为 4 行（配置地址合并、删除写死的「缓存策略」行）；「配置状态」仅异常时出现；按钮组精简为「强制刷新 / 校验并应用 / 恢复默认 / 下载默认 registry.json」，「检查条目可达性」从 GUI 移除（`registry-diagnose` API 保留）。
- **文案修正**：自定义源说明明确「整体替换主清单、仅影响『精选』区」；删除与实际不符的 TTL 写死描述（`timeoutMs`/`cacheTtlMin` 仍走 config 配置）。
- **dsh-m 自身**：本地 dev 版领先 npm 发布时改显「本地为开发版」而非误导性的旧「npm 最新」（新增 `ahead` 字段）。
- **关于**：文案对齐当前定位，新增 GitHub 仓库与问题反馈链接。

### 0.7.10 美化

- **头部三段分组**：标题与 tab 导航之间加发丝竖分隔线，「标题 | 导航 | 状态+窗口控制」边界清晰。
- **版本角标同色**：v 版本号不再用主文字亮色，与 dsh-m 名称统一为次级灰。
- **垂直节奏收敛**：窗口控制组按钮 26→28px 与 tab 按钮等高；版本角标微调至 24px 高；最大化/还原图标统一 12px；标题字重 700→600。

### 0.7.9 修复

- **分类 chips 顺序恒定**：退役「激活分类置前」的换序逻辑（此前每点一个被折叠裁掉的分类，它就会跳到首位，顺序随点击不断变化）。替换为：激活分类落在收起态裁剪区时自动展开完整分类行——「当前激活的分类始终可见」目标不变，顺序从此稳定；同一激活分类下手动收起会被尊重，换选其他被裁掉的分类或点「全部」时重置。

### 0.7.8 变更

- **修复「最大化没作用」的根因——CSS 热更自愈**：面板样式表此前只在首次注入（`#dshm-css` 存在即跳过），服务热更后旧 bundle 留下的样式表不含新类名规则（全屏/窗口控制组），导致新功能「点了没反应」、按钮裸奔成原生样式。现给注入的样式表带内容哈希（djb2）版本标记，bundle 更新后重新打开面板即自动替换旧样式，无需刷新页面。
- **窗口控制组重新配色**：去掉浮起底色，改透明底 + 发丝外框（与搜索清除钮同一配色语言），单格加宽 34→44px 防误触；关闭悬停仍为红色警示。

### 0.7.7 变更

- **移植 dsh-market 的全屏功能**：面板头部新增「最大化/还原」（连体窗口控制组设计：最大化 + 关闭等宽两格、发丝分隔线、统一 SVG 线条图标；关闭悬停红色警示）。全屏铺满视口、去圆角，状态 localStorage 记忆，Esc 关面板语义不变。
- 0.7.6 补记：版本角标去粗体、去点击复制（改静态展示）；吸顶分类行上沿镂空修复（sticky 锚点上移抵消容器 padding）；关闭/清除按钮改框线平面风。

### 0.7.5 变更

- **头部版本 chip 改显 dsh-m 自身版本**（`dsh-m v0.7.5`，点击复制；DSH 运行版本看设置页与 `dshm ping`）。
- **× 关闭/清除按钮统一重绘**：搜索清除、详情 Modal 关闭、操作记录行移除三处改用 SVG 线条图标 + 悬停浅底的专用按钮样式；搜索清除钮悬浮于输入框右缘（胶囊内对齐）。
- **修复 dsh-market 的 peer 告警**：`@deepseek-ai/dsh-tools` peer 由 `*`（semver 严格口径不匹配 rc 预发布版本）改为显式 range `^0.1.7-rc.2 || ^0.2.0-rc.1 || >=0.2.0`；未来更新的 rc 线（如 0.3.0-rc.x）需再追加。

### 0.7.4 变更

- **面板 tab 回退旧版分段按钮风格**（0.7.2 误改下划线样式，按主人要求还原圆角按钮组 + 高亮态）。
- **搜索框通长**：修复搜索容器缺 `display:flex` 导致输入框未拉伸的问题，恢复整行宽度。
- **筛选按钮与页号跳转控件重新配色**：筛选按钮改浮起面板底色 + 方角与分类 chips 区分（激活态品牌色描边）；页号输入改胶囊形细描边，「跳转」用品牌色文字钮。
- **吸顶分类行彻底不透**：背景直接使用不透明底色 token（此前的 color-mix 半透明配方在深色主题下仍会透出下方卡片文字），保留底部分隔线。

### 0.7.3 变更（含 0.7.2）

- **首页布局复刻 dsh-market**：市场页改为「分区 chips → 整宽搜索行 → 分类 chips + 行尾筛选弹层」结构；筛选弹层独立样式（方角矩形 + 前置 chevron），收纳排序字段（npm 下载量/Star 数/收录日期）、排列方向与每页条数（原排序下拉与分页器条数选择退役）。
- **收藏卡可点开详情**：修复收藏区卡片点击无响应——收藏快照字段不全，打开时按 id 从两分区内存 → market API → 快照三级解析完整条目，详情 Modal 与安装链路（含兼容确认）全量复用。
- **翻页页号跳转**：页码行新增页号输入框，输入有效页号回车或点「跳转」直达。
- **吸顶分类行毛玻璃**：滚动时吸顶的分类行加 backdrop blur 与底部分隔线，下方卡片文字不再透出干扰。
- **按需求移除**：「发现社区/申请收录」行（dsh-m 不支持收录功能）、「任务」按钮（操作记录面板恢复常驻）、「刷新」按钮（强制刷新在设置页）。
- 说明：收录条目不携带宿主版本要求字段，dsh-market 的「宿主版本」筛选项无数据源，未复刻。

### 0.7.1 修复

- **社区条目可安装**：修复 0.7.0 回归——社区区里的条目点安装报「registry 中没有该条目」（安装按收录 id 只查主清单，未查社区目录）；现与升级路径同构，主清单 miss 时按 id 查社区目录再装。
- **市场页第一行紧凑化**：搜索框、刷新、排序（社区区）并入分区 chips 行右侧；信息性来源横幅（「官方默认收录清单 / 自定义收录清单 / 来源为本地缓存」）退役——其「共 {count} 条」计数从未接线（恒显 0）；错误态「收录清单不可用」与社区兜底/陈旧提示保留。

### 0.7.0 新增

- **分区制市场**（[ADR-0004](./docs/adr/0004-zoned-market-display.md)）：数据层双清单合并不变，展示层按「社区（默认）/ 精选 / 收藏」三分区呈现；`dshm_search` 与 `dshm search` 改用 `--source community|primary|all` + `--offset` 真翻页（默认 10 条），`primary_only` 退役。
- **搜索相关性**：NFKC 归一化 + 中西文边界 + 字段加权（name/npm > owner > 描述 > 分类 > tags），多词同字段全命中；id 整串精确匹配最高优先。
- **操作记录 + 恢复执行器**、**本地收藏 + 下架清理**、**详情 Modal + 截图灯箱**、社区卡 byline/deprecated 徽章/目录版本快照兜底（不参与 outdated 判定）。

### 0.4.0 新增

- **开关（Enablement Toggle）**：已装卡片一键启停，内部自动路由行覆盖（单行插件，即时生效）或 Bundle 选择（多行插件）；写路径委派官方 `pluginManager` 服务、缺席时降级 loader 直操作（[ADR-0001](./docs/adr/0001-delegate-with-fallback-for-plugin-manager.md)）。
- **运行相位徽标**：loader fiber 状态投影，failed 一眼可见。
- **保护名单**：`dsh-m` 自身与官方宿主命脉 16 项不可开关、不可卸载（升级不受影响）。
- **精确构建放行**：needs-builds 拦截后按 pnpm 待决名单逐键放行，全量放行降为兜底并如实标注（[ADR-0002](./docs/adr/0002-precise-build-approval.md)）。
- **peer 兼容预检**：安装/升级前校验 `@deepseek-ai/dsh(-*)` peers 与运行时版本（GitHub 源明示未检）；不兼容时 GUI 弹确认、agent 回结构化结果、CLI `--force`。
- **实测版本清单（verified）**：registry 条目可选 `verified` 数组记录实测过的 DSH 运行时版本——实测声明而非预测声明，只展示不拦截。
- **bundle 身份验证**：装后检测无补丁层的包并警告「已装入为纯依赖」。

### 0.4.x 退役

- **元数据源竞速**：0.4.0 曾引入 npmjs / npmmirror ping 竞速选择元数据读取源，现整体移除（含 `probeEnabled` / `probeTimeoutMs` / `probeCacheTtlMin` 三个设置项与设置页展示）。官方同款探测只服务于「安装对话框 registry 默认预选」，dsh-m 无此交互；实测宿主机 npmjs 稳定更快，探测恒等默认行为。元数据读取固定走 npmjs，与安装链路（profile `.npmrc` 默认源）一致。

## English

### 0.10.22 Feature: community browse mirror — displaced entries stay visible + "Curated" badge (search & install semantics unchanged)

- **Background**: the primary-first merge semantics dropped entries collected by BOTH the curated registry and the community catalog from the community zone — unfindable by paging (owner, 2026-10-27). Shipped via an approved design (docs/specs/2026-10-27-community-zone-displaced-entries-design.md) + two independent review rounds (10 plan issues + 4 implementation issues, all closed).
- **Changes**: ① `mergeRegistries` retains displaced entries with a `primaryOverlap` mark (judgment order: primary identity clash before community-internal dedup; primaryNpm before seenNpm within the npm segment — segment-order reading ① anchored; community-internal duplicates still dropped and counted separately); ② `mergedIndex` identity-resolution domain excludes marked entries — findById/lookupInstalled contracts intact, three regression anchors green with zero modifications (install/upgrade paths untouched); ③ `listMarket` search pool excludes marked entries — browse mirrors, search unchanged; ④ client "Curated" badge on community cards (violet variant, distinct from npm indigo) + cardOf key domain-split to avoid duplicate-key collisions for id-displaced mirror cards; ⑤ the "Community" badge now renders only in cross-zone search results (tautological in the community browse view, and badge crowding squeezed card names — live feedback).
- **Also**: GLOSSARY gains "让位（Displacement）"; the identityKey content-fingerprint note remains an independent soft item, not in this release.
- **Verification**: full npm test **1791/1791** (4 new marking cases + 3 view cases + badge wiring source anchor; three identity regression anchors green with zero modifications; red-light replay 5 red / 10 green), typecheck 0; golden 8 scenarios reviewed against the three-category whitelist (browse items+counts, installed warnings, market-search zero change); live link-mode experience passed.

### 0.10.21 Feature: icon resolution chain — declared icon → avatar fallback → letter placeholder (ADR-0025); installed panel byte-identical with the official panel

- **Background**: the owner asked for "GitHub avatars demoted to fallback, declared icons on par with the official installed panel", worried about rendering performance across thousands of plugins (scale facts: server-side pagination 32/page, cap 96, ≤96 icons per viewport — pure front-end lazy loading suffices; no proxy, no pre-crawl). Shipped via a grill-with-docs consensus → implementation plan → two independent agent review rounds (10+4 issues all closed, 1 blocker); decisions and known deviations in `docs/adr/0025-icon-resolution-chain.md`.
- **Changes**: ① chain: new `installed-icon.ts` replicates the official iconOf semantics (relative-path enforcement, two-sided realpath containment — proven against a symlink-installed plugin, extension whitelist, 256KiB double check, isFile; exports `<pkg>/icon` best-effort); the installed panel drops its hardwired `icon:null` and now consumes host-equivalent data URIs; ② curated registry: the `icon` field goes live (https URLs only — enforced by validateRegistry as-is + client-side defense + a guard test), populated for 16/23 entries (jsDelivr exact-version pins, each HEAD-verified 200 + image/*); market/curated/community pages join the local data URI for page-matched installed entries (page-bounded disk reads); ③ performance: icon IO lazy loading (IntersectionObserver rootMargin 200px + `loading=lazy`, 8s timer gating reused from the screenshot precedent), zero added IO on the market query hot path; ④ visuals: `object-fit:contain`, themed plate behind img icons (contrast boost for thin-stroke SVGs), letter-state gradient retained, 40×40 container unchanged; ⑤ resilience: silent candidate-to-candidate degradation, unconditional hooks (prevents conditional-hook crashes on reused installed-tab instances), out-of-range candidates fold into the letter placeholder.
- **Verification**: full npm test **1783/1783** (new installed-icon (two-sided realpath + symlink fixtures) / client-icon-view / registry-icon-url https guard / market·installed wiring tests), typecheck 0; live smoke 12 of 15 installed plugins emit declared icons (criterion ≥7); pre-release independent reviewer ran three rounds live (1779→1781→1783 all green) with red-light re-enactments.

### 0.10.20 Fix: reconcile-re-judged rows losing version transition (enqueue-time `from` persistence + reconcile back-fill of `meta.to`) + explicit "same-runtime" contract for the golden corpus

- **Symptom (2026-10-26, live incident)**: after a panel-initiated dsh-m self-upgrade was interrupted by its own bundle-swap reload, the operation record got re-judged as done by the restore reconciliation — but the row showed no version transition at all ("升级 dsh-m 15m29s", both old and new versions missing); on a peer machine the same day, Agent-tool channel records and a 5-second panel upgrade all displayed `vOld→vNew` normally.
- **Root cause**: the 0.10.3 restore-reconcile branch only writes `toVersion`/`reconciledAfterRestart`; the display keys `meta.from`/`meta.to` are only ever written when the pump merges opMeta from the executor's successful return (0.9.75) — and a dsh-m self-upgrade kills its own panel, interrupting that final write. Enqueue only persisted the comparison key `toVersion` (0.10.3); `from` was known at enqueue time but never stored, and is unrecoverable after the restart.
- **Changes**: ① the three upgrade sites (version chip / installed list / market card) now persist `from` = the installed version at enqueue time (`version` / `it.version` / `it.installedVersion`) — the old version survives any restart; ② the `restoreRecords` re-judge branch back-fills `meta.to` with the installed version (`opRowVm` renders vOld→vNew from from/to only); legacy leftovers without `from` get no key (display falls back to "→vNew"); the failure path leaves meta untouched; ③ four unit tests (reconcile back-fill / no-key for legacy leftovers / opRowVm end-to-end vOld→vNew regression anchor / failure path writes nothing) + the 0.10.3 three-site source anchors refreshed.
- **Side fix (tests/search-relevance)**: the golden frozen corpus went red on Unicode 16.0-data runtimes (node22+ICU77) with 2/3888 cases (U+0CDC / U+088F) — both code points are only assigned as letters since Unicode 17.0, so `\p{L}` drifts with the runtime's ICU data; this is within the implementation's documented "same predicate, same runtime" contract, not implementation drift (node24+ICU78 measures 20/20 green on the same fixture). The test is now scoped by `process.versions.unicode`: pinned whenever it matches the CI baseline (node24/17.0), skipped with a reason (including the regeneration command) otherwise — fixture and implementation untouched.
- **Verification**: targeted suites 131/131; full `npm test` **1755 pass + 1 skipped (runtime-scoped) + 0 fail** (measured on node22.21.1); typecheck clean; cross-runtime comparison (node22.21.1/ICU77.1 vs node24.20.0/ICU78.3) plus a stash comparison (clean main is equally red) doubly rule out an implementation regression.

### 0.10.19 Dependency major-version adaptation: undici 8 absolute-form proxy + TS 7 no-JS-API guard bridge (Dependabot #16/#17 debt cleared, issue #20)

- **Background**: two red items from Dependabot's first wave of major-version bumps were parked in issue #20: undici 7.30.0→8.12.3 (PR #16, 2/1752 failures all in the proxy path) and typescript ^5.6.0→7.0.2 (PR #17, check-client-imports crash + typecheck false green). The adaptation landed via PR #21 and went through two independent review rounds (adversarial deep review + delta re-check) to a unified approve.
- **Changes**: ① undici 8's `EnvHttpProxyAgent` now sends absolute-form requests for http targets (standard forward-proxy semantics; https still uses CONNECT) — "reached via proxy" semantics are unchanged and the production hand-off layer needed no fork; the test fake proxy was upgraded to a dual-dialect real forwarding proxy (CONNECT byte relay + true absolute-form forwarding to the origin), keeping assertions version-agnostic, verified green under both undici 7 and 8; ② TypeScript 7 (Go-native rewrite) no longer ships an in-process JS API, so the guard's AST parsing now rides a new devDep `typescript6` (`npm:typescript@^6.0.2` alias, the TS 6 bridge line) while the `tsc` CLI uses the `typescript@7` native binary — typecheck turns from false green into a real gate; ③ `engines.node` `>=22` → `>=22.19.0` (aligns with undici 8.12.3's engines floor, closing the declaration gap); ④ three defensive tightenings on the dual-dialect stub: malformed absolute-form URLs answer 400 without counting a hit, the fwd error callback guards `headersSent` before writing 502, and an upstream response error destroys the client connection.
- **Verification**: `npm ci` with zero ERESOLVE → `npm test` **1752/1752** green (including both previously red cases) → `typecheck` clean; build output is byte-identical to baseline (cross-compiler same-source comparison: all 49 lib files sha256-equal); CI + Registry check workflows green; the guard shows zero parsing drift between TS 5.9.3 and 6.0.3.

### 0.10.18 Hardening: supply-chain security — all GitHub Actions pinned to SHAs, SECURITY.md + dependabot added, check-release moved to spawnSync, thin root entry host.js (awesome-ai-plugins source scan 64→100)

- **Background**: dsh-m entered listing review at hashgraph-online/awesome-ai-plugins, whose centralized source scan (plugin-scanner, threshold 80) scored the repository 64/100 (1 high + 7 medium + 2 low).
- **Changes**: ① all five `@v4` refs in `.github/workflows/publish.yml` / `registry.yml` pinned to commit SHAs (tag noted in comments) — a marketplace plugin is itself part of the distribution chain, so CI references must be immutable; ② added `SECURITY.md` (GitHub private vulnerability reporting, no public issues) and `.github/dependabot.yml` (weekly github-actions + npm ecosystems); ③ `scripts/check-release.mjs` replaced `execSync(\`git rev-parse "v${version}^{commit}"\`)` with `spawnSync('git', ['rev-parse', …])` array args — removes the shell-injection HIGH finding with equivalent behavior (non-zero exit / missing git still leaves the field empty for the ✗ verdict; only stderr spill differs); ④ added a thin root entry `host.js` (re-exporting `name`/`inject`/`Config`/`apply` from `lib/host.js`), with `main` and `exports["."]` pointing at it and the file added to the publish surface — static scans of a fresh clone (no built `lib/`) can now see the Cordis apply export (root-cures `DSH_RUNTIME_APPLY_MISSING`); npm installs behave identically (`prepare` still builds lib); ⑤ reworded one docs/plans line that named the full-access sandbox mode verbatim (matched the scanner's approval-bypass-default rule).
- **Verification**: full npm test 1752/1752 + typecheck clean; plugin-scanner re-run on a fresh clone scores **100/100 with zero findings**; the assert-pack gate was checked to accept the new `host.js` in the pack manifest (REQUIRED list intact, no forbidden matches).

### 0.10.17 Fix: marketplace pager scroll-to-top — the viewport returns to the top of the list on page move / filter change / zone switch (root-cures the stuck sticky-anchor no-op; plan C, three adversarial review rounds)

- **Symptom (observed 2026-10-25)**: in the marketplace tab, paginating via the numbered pager (at the list bottom) left the viewport at the bottom — the top-ranked items of the new page were invisible first, inverting reading order; category / page-size / sort / search changes that reset to page 1 from a deep position kept the viewport in place too.
- **Root cause**: the scroll-to-top shipped in 0.7.0 used `.dsvm-chipswrap` (a position:sticky header) as the scroll anchor — when paging, the user is always below the list top, so the stuck header is permanently visible and the browser misjudges it as "already in view" from its current (stuck) geometry, making the scroll a no-op (at most a one compensation-band nudge). The search-mode anchor `.dsvm-searchmeta` is non-sticky and always worked; one function, two paths, one broken — which pinned the diagnosis.
- **Fix (plan C; new GLOSSARY terms: scroll-to-top / page move / filter identity key / scroll container)**: ① explicit scroll-container ownership — the shell owns a `bodyRef` on `.dshm-body` and passes it to MarketTab as a prop; ② layer-1 page moves (page numbers / ‹ › / jump) scroll immediately via `scrollBodyTop(bodyRef.current, preferredPagerBehavior())` — a direct container scroll that depends on no child geometry, smooth with an instant fallback under `prefers-reduced-motion`; ③ layer-2 — a change of the filter identity key `filterScrollKey(zone, query)` (zone + query + category + sort + page size; `offset` explicitly excluded) triggers an instant pre-paint `useLayoutEffect` scroll; page moves never trip this layer, so the two are mutually exclusive with no double-scroll; ④ no-scroll whitelist — mutation reloads and background refreshes keep the key unchanged, preserving the scroll position; ⑤ shipped through three independent adversarial reviews (design / plan / implementation; the last closed at 0 blockers / 0 majors / 0 minors, with a red-light re-enactment proving the 12-red/32-green TDD claim verbatim).
- **Verification**: 12 new tests (8 filter-identity-key + 4 scroll primitives) via TDD red→green; full `npm test` **1752/1752** with zero failures; `typecheck` 0; repo-wide `scrollIntoView` exactly 2 (the openOps call + a 0.9.76 comment); build-level double review in an isolated copy (npm test / typecheck / smoke all green + the red-light re-enactment); live hot-install on the production profile (atomic client.js swap, fingerprint probe + live served-bytes evidence) with the owner's manual regression (smooth scroll-to-top on page move) passing.

### 0.10.16 Fix: generalize the post-write false-failure ledger reconciliation to all upgrade paths — reconcile to success when the target version is already written (skip-browser-auth 0.4.3 live incident)

- **Symptom (2026-10-25, live)**: a Desktop batch upgrade of @iasiv5/dsh-skip-browser-auth 0.4.2→0.4.3 reported failure (reason: already-installed, auto-reinstall incomplete) while the ledger and node_modules already held 0.4.3; restarting Desktop finished activation. 0.10.2 added ledger reconciliation only for the D3 self-upgrade channel; the regular batch path still reported failure.
- **Root cause**: the host 0.2.1 already-installed guard's self-referential artifact is channel-agnostic — installBundle writes all three ledgers (manifest/node_modules/lock) to the target version, then its own completion path re-trips the guard and returns failed; at that point the fallback-reinstall leg's assumption ("after remove the name is gone from the manifest") no longer holds, so the rollback re-trips the guard and misreports rollback-failed.
- **Fix (src/core/profile-ops.ts runManagedInstall)**: both failure gates (two-step second leg / first leg) now reconcile the ledger before declaring failure (mirrors D3 0.10.2): if the exact npm-source target version is already written, the upgrade factually holds — reconcile to success (needsRestart=true, restart Desktop to activate), never downgrade; if the ledger read fails or still shows the old version, the existing rollback/failure semantics are unchanged (degraded recovery and the absent-package hard-error family are intact).
- **Verification**: the profile-ops suite is now **78/78 green** — three new pins: T10 (second-leg artifact with all three ledgers at target → reconciled to success: zero rollback legs, target version recorded, needsRestart, output annotated), T11 (genuine second-leg failure with the old version still on the ledger → no reconciliation, degraded semantics intact), T12 (first-leg genuine failure: zero rollback, zero remove); GitHub-source specs (no version) skip the reconciliation branch. The 0.9.19 "ledger split re-read" pin turns green with the tightened condition (node_modules-new/manifest-old-pin keeps its wait-and-rollback-risk semantics — never mis-reconciled). Windows full suite: 1717 tests, 1690 pass, 17 fail — the 17 match this machine's pre-existing baseline (know-how 028 §4 families) name by name, zero new failures.

### Display name in 0.10.15 — official Plugins page title "dsh-m" → "Plugin Marketplace (dsh-m)"

- **Background**: the official DSH Plugins page resolves each card title as "exported locale `meta.title` → package.json → package name". `dshmarket` shows "插件市场" through `locale/zh.json`, while dsh-m shipped no locale files, so its title fell back to the bare package name `dsh-m` — asymmetric with its (Chinese) description.
- **Fix**: ① add `locale/zh.json` + `locale/en.json` (`meta.title` = "插件市场（dsh-m）" / "Plugin Marketplace (dsh-m)"; the Chinese description matches package.json's verbatim, the English one is a fresh translation); ② expose `./locale/*.json` in package.json exports and ship `locale` in the publish surface (`en.json` is the host's discovery anchor for the locale directory — without it the whole directory is skipped); ③ the assert-pack gate now lists both locale files as required release artifacts; ④ new `tests/plugin-meta-locale.test.mjs` guard (exports key, bilingual meta validity, Chinese title regression anchor, gate self-consistency).
- **Deployment**: the host's `readPluginMeta` reads fresh on every inventory build — after the upgrade, refresh the Plugins page; no restart needed.

### Fix in 0.10.14 — "Update all" batch upgrade: serial with fail-short-circuit, dsh-m self-upgrade ordered last (#10)

- **Symptom (#10, reproduced on 0.10.8 and 0.10.13)**: clicking "Update all" with two or more outdated plugins upgraded one successfully while the other failed with "命令失败 (exit 1): ERR_PNPM_… / profile may need manual repair"; retrying the failed item alone succeeded.
- **Root cause**: the batch entry fired every upgrade as a floating promise (no await, no short-circuit). The backend Profile-change transaction FIFO does serialize execution, but once dsh-m's self-upgrade committed, the host swapped and reloaded the market plugin itself — and the next item of the same batch ran its transaction and frozen re-verification against a just-changed profile, which fails by construction.
- **Fix**: ① the batch now awaits each item and stops at the first failure — the failing item's "which target + raw error" stays in its own toast and operation record (no longer drowned by siblings), and later items never run on a manual-repair half-repaired profile; superseded (benign skip) does not interrupt the batch. ② a single ordering source `batchUpgradeOrder` (pure, Node-testable) pins dsh-m last, so a self-swap never has same-batch followers; a bilingual hint ("dsh-m itself upgrades last: its self-swap won't interrupt the other items") appears next to the button. ③ `doUpgrade` returns per-item success: the single-item Upgrade button ignores it (zero behavior change) and transport semantics keep zero client special-casing (0.10.0 R6 unchanged).
- **Verification**: 5 pure-logic cases for `batchUpgradeOrder` + 5 structural anchors for the installed-tab batch wiring; full `npm test` 1,733/1,733 green.

### Copy update in 0.10.13 — self-built plugin compatibility metadata for DSH 0.2.1-rc.3

- **Market registry**: record that dsh-skins was tested on DSH 0.2.1-rc.3 in both Web and Desktop; add the Web rc.3 smoke results for skip-browser-auth and copilot-auth; remove DSH release numbers from the four decoupled entries while retaining plugin prerequisites.
- **Protected-list anchor**: update the source comment to dsh-plugin-manager 0.2.1-rc.3; the official 17-module set is unchanged.
- **Verification**: all 1,723 tests passed with `npm test`.

### Refactor in 0.10.12 — market read/write seams: write side extracted into market-mutation.ts (ADR-0024), zero behavior change

- **Need (2026-10-24 steelman architecture verdict + two grill consensus rounds + independent plan & implementation reviews, all closed)**: `market.ts` had grown to 1960 lines / 37 exports mixing reads and writes (a shallow module whose interface is nearly as wide as its implementation), with 51 touches in two months — the #1 core hotspot; the 0.10.11 community-issue batch required cross-module chain-reading for the #3/#6 same-root-cause fix, evidence of failed locality.
- **Approach (ADR-0024)**: the write side (install/uninstall/upgrade/self-upgrade, guard wiring, mutation session, prior snapshots) moved verbatim into `src/core/market-mutation.ts` (new glossary term "Market Mutation"), while the read side (listing/search/installed projection/probe budget/sorting) stays in market.ts; one-way dependency mutation→read; all callers (5 in src, 11 in tests) re-import directly with zero re-exports, also retiring the ADR-0016 merged-market forwarding debt (matchInstalledByEntry / communityOutcome / CommunityRegistrySummary now imported directly); tests split into `tests/market-mutation.test.mjs`.
- **Verification**: typecheck clean; the verbatim red line verified by an independent reviewer via an LF-normalized empty diff (960 lines byte-identical); full suite identical to baseline at 1700/1672/18 (all 18 failures are the pre-existing Windows symlink-EPERM family — zero new failures, zero test loss: 116 cases conserved as 84+32); check-release green except the release-time tag≠HEAD assertion. Zero user-visible behavior or copy changes.
- **Release note**: purely internal restructuring, no behavioral fixes — upgrading is optional; it significantly lowers the comprehension and testing cost of future marketplace-side changes. Implementation plan: `docs/plans/2026-10-24-market-read-write-seams-implementation-plan.md`.

### Fix in 0.10.11 — community issue batch: carrier false-disabled / desktop kind rejection / core-package shadowing / codex-ui entry missing (#2/#3+#6/#7/#4)

- **Need (five GitHub community reports since 2026-10-20)**: ① the Installed list falsely reported carrier-bundle plugins (dsh-ops, dsh-complex-project-preset) as disabled while they were running (#2); ② on desktop distros whose profile is not named `desktop` (dsh-tauri uses `tauri`, dataelement/dsh-desktop uses `web`), write operations were refused or took the wrong path into the install guard (#3/#6, same root cause); ③ declaring @deepseek-ai/dsh-atomic-write in dependencies got it hoisted over the app-bundled core package — the official health check flagged danger and the launcher blocked restart (#7); ④ the marketplace entry disappeared when dsh-codex-ui was enabled (#4).
- **Root cause & fix**: ① entry claiming matched name === pkg only — mirrors the official dshmarket ownsLoaderEntry/carriedRowLive rule: exact-or-subpath names, plus claiming by the package's own patch insert ids (include-prefix stripped, dual id projection); ② `kindOf()` enumerated host kind by name and must leak — `tauri` joins the desktop name set, and host-sourced profiles gain a generation-projection fact probe (node_modules symlinks into .generations, or lockfile link: evidence ⇒ desktop delegation to the official manager; fallback hosts are never probed, zero drift; deliberately NOT "pluginManager presence" — official Web hosts provide it too), derivePrior/verifyNpmCommitState accept projections (generation dirs are self-contained rollback snapshots), WRITE_GUIDANCE empty strings and the dangling guard.noforce copy now give an actionable exit; ③ dsh-atomic-write vendored into `core/atomic-write.ts` per the report's option 2 (MIT, lock protocol byte-faithful), removed from dependencies with a dependency-hygiene guard test; ④ the shell-takeover sentinel is tiered (shell-takeover.js): cooperative shells (whose footer renders the footer.action slot; allowlist includes codex-ui NS) keep the entry, hostile shells (ds-bot class) still yield.
- **Verification**: typecheck clean; on a DSH 0.2.1-rc.3 web host (link install) at the anchored SHA (9b80477), 1719/1719 tests with zero failures and a seven-item compatibility checklist all green (kind not misrouted / five-step write path / installed reconciliation / lock & atomic write / panel entry / CLI / doctor with zero danger verdicts); on Windows, the 17 failures match the pre-existing environment baseline name-for-name. Release note: #3/#6/#7 require upgrading to this version; a brand-new dataelement profile (no projection evidence yet) still installs via the web path.

### Fix in 0.10.10 — sidebar bilingual label registration silently dead (locale.register references undefined identifiers after dictionary extraction)

- **Symptom**: since 0.10.9, the locale dictionary registration for the sidebar dsh-m entry silently fails — `locale.register("dshm", { zh: ZH, en: EN })` in `main.jsx` references undeclared identifiers after the i18n dictionary moved out (ADR-0023 knife 1, T1), and a `try/catch` swallows the ReferenceError; the Chinese UI shows nothing wrong, while the English UI falls back to default sidebar labels.
- **Caught by**: the client free-variable guard shipped in the 0.10.9 retro (`scripts/check-client-imports.mjs`, TS-AST identifier closure scan, wired into the `npm test` chain and inherited by registry CI) — first run hit it. The landmine was unreachable through SSR, tests, CI, and on-machine dogfood alike: a live demonstration of the "first-render ceiling" blind spot.
- **Fix**: destructure `ZH, EN` back from the require (`i18n.js` already exports them); the same batch lands retro C1-C5: the free-variable guard in the test chain, a CHANGELOG count-freshness step in check-release (top entry's claimed count asserted against a live run), and "vacuous-criterion" + "move-script invariant" lines in CODING-STANDARDS.
- **Verification**: full suite 1671/1671 green; guard red-green verified both ways (injected fake variable → exit 1 with the offender named, revert → green); check-release count step claim = measured.

### Refactor in 0.10.9 — client shell component-seam campaign (ADR-0023): main.jsx 4475→1163 lines, zero behavior change

- **Need (2026-10-22 steelman architecture ruling + 2026-10-23 grill consensus of 19 decisions, independent review of 16 findings)**: the main.jsx monolith (4,475 lines, 113 touches in the past month) had "grep the source text" as its test surface (18 test files reading the source, 11+ source-assertion describes); the reconciliation-banner theme alone took four fix rounds, each touching 3-5 scattered regions — "the interface is the source text" was the institutionalized cost of a shallow interface.
- **Approach (three knives, ADR-0023, merged into one release)**: ① i18n dictionaries and CSS extracted to `i18n.js`/`styles.js` (ESM named exports, pure moves); ② `panel-ops.js` deep module — explicit bind/unbind replaces the module-level mutable pump-context singleton, `planReconcileActions` pure planning function (compute-then-write extended to the orchestration layer), `restartBannerDecision` single source for all five banner sites (delegating to toggleNoticeKeys/upgradeNotify instead of reimplementing), refreshViews dead field removed; ③ eleven .jsx component files + `hooks.js` (api/useMarketData/useScrollableContain etc.), main.jsx reduced to a composition root (MarketPanel + mount + apply + tool views, 1,163 lines).
- **Source-anchor policy (three buckets)**: "wherever a seam exists, test through the interface; source anchors remain only for physical facts with no seam" — all 195 anchors migrated per-row (appendix timing-binding table: content and its anchors move in the same task/commit); slice anchors retired into single-file full-text anchors, cross-file counts became multi-file aggregates (mutationFailed consumer-set directory level, degraded dual-tab aggregate), negative key anchors scan dictionary side and call side; the i18n audit's caller side extended to every component file; the render-smoke copy-and-append-export hack retired (only MarketPanel remains) with a dual direct-require entry plus props-variant expansion.
- **Verification**: full suite 1671/1671 green (baseline 1645 + 10 from the i18n audit caller-side expansion + 11 panel-ops direct tests + 2 T12 variants + 3 title-hygiene guard tests; arithmetic corrected per reviews O-3 and O-5); the multi-sourced render probe re-run showed zero long tasks across all scenarios at execution-time machine state (on the review-recheck day the same machine showed a single boot-segment long task even at the pre-campaign baseline cb0f295 — the band is machine-state sensitive, so the equivalence criterion is the **same-day same-machine baseline comparison**: HEAD matches the baseline band, cards/icons/tabs/commits structural counts are identical, and S2/S4/S5/S7 are faster), max 60.7ms < the 65ms perception line — the split has zero render regression; triple self-checks (opsPumpCtx gone / onMutation: refreshViews exactly 2 / pump creation moved) plus per-knife move self-checks all pass. Two SSR-blind-spot landmines found and fixed during execution (OP_STATUS_CLS/PHASE_LABEL free variables and a lost dsvmModalDepth declaration, both caught via the probe/browser path — a live demonstration of the "first-render ceiling").
- **Governance**: GLOSSARY gains the "Panel Ops Wiring" term; CODING-STANDARDS "客户端状态机约定" rewritten (panel-ops precedent + components importable by tests + three-bucket policy + no-jsdom discipline); ADR-0020 probe anchors multi-sourced alongside the split. Implementation plan (with the 195-row anchor disposition table): `docs/plans/2026-10-23-client-shell-seams-implementation-plan.md`.

### Fix in 0.10.8 — enablement read path misreports multi-entry packages as disabled (skip-browser-auth dual-generation gating hits first-match)

- **Need (2026-10-19, owner)**: the official plugin page showed `@iasiv5/dsh-skip-browser-auth` ON while the dsh-m installed panel showed it as disabled — books vs. reality split (token-less `GET /` returned 200 and the journal carried the `BrowserAuth has been skipped` banner, proving the plugin was actually running; the dsh-m reading was the wrong one).
- **Root cause**: skip-auth 0.4.x ships a dual-generation self-gating design that registers two same-named loader entries (`trusted-connection` webRuntime row + `trusted-connection-021` webStartup row), each with a `!!js` gate so exactly one row is alive per host; the dsh-m read path (`findLoaderEntry`) matched by package name and took the **first** row — on 0.2.1-rc.x that is the dormant row (disabled:true), so the whole package was misread as disabled (the official readPluginInventory aggregates differently and showed the truth).
- **Approach**: `composeEnablement` now collects all matching entries (`findLoaderEntries`) — `enabled = any row !disabled`, with phase projected from the first enabled row's fiber; single-entry semantics unchanged (locked by regression tests). The write path was already multi-entry safe (host mode delegates to the official `setBundleEnabled` and re-reads with `.some()`) and is untouched. Also fixed a stale assertion: PROTECTED_MODULES official 17 items + dsh-m = 18 (the expectation was missed when `3239460` added `dsh-api-remote-access`; a stash comparison proved it was not a regression from this change).
- **Verification**: new dual-entry regression tests (one dormant one live → enabled+active; all dormant → false+null); enablement+installed 55/55 green, full suite 0 failures; on-machine hot-deploy verification returned `enabled:true, phase:"active"` from `/dshm installed`, consistent with the official page after restart.

### Fixes in 0.10.7 — reconciliation completion banner deduplicated once (independent seen-set + persist-before-notify) + 0.10.6 retro engineering groundwork

- **Need (2026-10-18, owner)**: after the 0.10.5→0.10.6 upgrade, the restore-reconciliation completion banner popped on **every panel open** ("now at v0.10.6") — by its 0.10.4 charter ("a success must not be silent") it should notify once; the every-open root cause (the done write-through being knocked back to running by a resurrection path on the real machine) remains undiagnosed (not reproducible in local tests).
- **Approach**: ① **Plan A (owner ruling, ADR-0022 decision 8)**: the seen-set lives in an independent localStorage key `dshm-reconcile-notified` (members are `id|target|toVersion` tuples) — outside the record's lifecycle, robust to the resurrection path; ② order reversed: `replaceAll` persists first and only a successful persist notifies — a persist throw silently forfeits that one notification and leaves a console.error diagnostic (losing one reminder beats repeated bombardment); ③ the failure/not-completed warn banner is not deduplicated (host-api memoization already rate-limits it per process) — asymmetric notify: success notifies once, failure must sound; ④ the resurrection mechanism is a separately tracked hunt, not blocking this release.
- **Also in this release (the four 0.10.6 retro commits, already on main)**: source-anchor hygiene guard (mechanical guard against the CRLF×multi-line-needle always-true class + the C3 legacy anchor fixed); i18n audit extended to op-outcome.js; assert-pack wired into the release gate (7th check); `scripts/release.mjs` release orchestration (`npm run release`); CODING-STANDARDS anchor-discipline third fact + describe-helpers review item; know-how AGENTS.md GITHUB_TOKEN hint.
- **Verification**: typecheck clean; full suite 1620 tests (new: seen-set tolerance quadruple + roundtrip + order-reversal/diagnostic/wiring source anchors; pre-existing failures dropped to 17 = the 028 set minus C3, which is now green).

### Fixes in 0.10.6 — restore-reconciliation and boot-reconciliation match relaxed to "reached or passed" (rc-aware) — lagging records closed out

- **Need (2026-10-18, pending item from the 0.10.5 dogfood wrap-up)**: real-machine event — the interrupted leftover of the 0.10.3→0.10.4 upgrade (meta.toVersion 0.10.4) was only swept by the restore reconciliation after 0.10.5 had been activated, but the match used strict equality (installed 0.10.5 ≠ recorded 0.10.4) so it never hit, and the leftover kept nagging; meanwhile the banner's `to` referenced the record's own historical target (0.10.4), misaligned with the actually installed version (0.10.5).
- **Approach**: ① new `reachedVersion(installed, target)` (rc-aware semantic ≥ comparison, pure, exported from operations.js) — the restore-reconciliation match relaxed to "installed has reached or passed meta.toVersion"; on a hit, meta.toVersion is set to the actually installed version (the banner's copy source no longer lags). ② the host-api boot reconciliation relaxed the same way (`isNewerVersion`, rc-aware): installed ≥ intent target closes out as ok. ③ reachedVersion exported + unit tests (equal/newer/older/prerelease ordering/invalid shapes — five groups).
- **Verification**: typecheck clean; full suite 1612 tests (7 new: five reachedVersion groups + restore-reconciliation lagging close-out + boot-reconciliation relaxed hit; 18 pre-existing failures are the 028 machine-environment set, zero new).

### Fixes in 0.10.5 — missing restore-reconciliation banner copy key (raw-key toast) + i18n completeness audit test

- **Need (2026-10-18, real-machine dogfood of 0.10.4)**: after the 0.10.3→0.10.4 self-upgrade restart, the panel toast showed the raw key `upgrade.reconciled.done` for a few seconds — the restore-reconciliation banner's copy key was never defined (lookup falls back to the key itself); this defect class had no test coverage (parametrized lookup calls were outside the existing source anchors).
- **Approach**: ① add the bilingual `upgrade.reconciled.done` key (restore-reconciliation confirmation banner copy); ② add `tests/client-i18n.test.mjs` making the i18n completeness audit a permanent test — every `lookup("key")` literal in main.jsx (including parametrized calls; dynamic-prefix keys exempt) must exist in the dictionaries, preventing this regression class.
- **Verification**: the audit test, before the fix, flagged exactly this one key as the sole miss among 178 literal keys, and went green after; typecheck clean; full suite with zero new failures against the 028 baseline.

### Fixes + features in 0.10.4 — published-surface slimming (DESIGN.md/CHANGELOG.md removed) + assert-pack old/new npm compatibility + D3 reconciliation completion banner

- **Need**: ① the npm package's Code page carried the full `docs/DESIGN.md` design document and the 343 KB `CHANGELOG.md` (≈76% of the tarball) — repository/collaboration assets with zero runtime consumers that should not be on the published surface (owner); ② 0.10.3 dogfood pending item: a reconciled self-upgrade success is silent (the banner dies with the old panel during the host's own reload) and must be re-reported on the next panel open.
- **Approach**: ① both documents removed from the package.json `files` allowlist (tarball measured 450 KB → 303 KB, −33%); assert-pack gains a reverse assertion against regression + incidental compatibility fix for the new npm top-level shape (old bare array `[...]` → new `{"<name>": {...}}`); DESIGN.md §6 self-description amended. ② the host-api self-check reconciliation payload now surfaces `intentVersion`/`fromVersion`; the chip consumes `intentResolved` to raise the completion banner (ok → "self-upgrade finished (confirmed by boot reconciliation)", not-completed → warn "target not installed, retry available"); the restore flow raises a success banner for upgrade records re-judged as done (meta.reconciledAfterRestart).
- **Verification**: `npm pack --dry-run --json | assert-pack` green (52 files, both documents absent, reverse assertion in place); typecheck clean; full suite (new pins: three restore-reconciliation shapes, three site toVersion anchors, reconciliation banner structure/copy keys, T-D3 applied core-level pin; 18 pre-existing failures are the 028 machine-environment set, zero new).
- **Also in this release**: compatibility info refreshed to DSH 0.2.1-rc.2 (README badge/compat table/registry dsh-m entry description); GitHub Release badge removed (versions follow the npm badge); registry dsh-skins entry copy & verified refresh folded in (with the 1.5.8 Desktop adaptation); the 0.2.1 guard-adaptation implementation plan document added to the repo (`docs/plans/2026-10-17-host-021-already-installed-adaptation-implementation-plan.md`).

### Fixes in 0.10.3 — operation-pump restore ledger reconciliation (pending item 1) + D3 applied core-level pin (pending item 3)

- **Need (2026-10-18, pending items from the 0.10.2 dogfood wrap-up)**: a panel reload during the host's own bundle swap interrupts the pump's final ledger write, and the leftover running record is unconditionally labeled「进程重启中断」(process-restart interrupted) by the restore flow — in the 0.10.2 self-upgrade event the upgrade had actually succeeded (intent ledger resolved ok + all three ledgers written through) yet was recorded as failed, misleading the audit trail. Separately: D3 `applied → needsRestart=false` lacked a core-level pin (previously covered only indirectly at the client layer; review pending item 3).
- **Approach**: ① `restoreRecords` gains a third parameter `installedVersionOf` (an account-reality re-read seam implemented in main.jsx via `api("installed")`, same live-read discipline as stillApplies) — an upgrade-kind running leftover whose ledger has reached `meta.toVersion` is re-judged as done (`meta.reconciledAfterRestart` recorded); anything else keeps the interrupted semantics; omitting the third parameter preserves the old behavior (zero migration for existing callers). ② the three upgrade sites now carry `toVersion` in the enqueue meta (chip=state.latest, installed list=it.latestVersion, market card=it.latest) — the comparison basis for the restore reconciliation.
- **Verification**: typecheck clean; full suite (6 new: T-D3 applied core-level pin + three restore-reconciliation shapes + three site meta anchors; 18 pre-existing failures are the 028 machine-environment set, zero new).

### Fixes in 0.10.2 — account-reality reconciliation for D3 self-upgrade post-write pseudo-failure + silent self-upgrade (first D3 dogfood round)

- **Need (2026-10-18 early morning, first real-machine D3 dogfood — the 0.10.0→0.10.1 self-upgrade event)**: the self-upgrade reported failure (`application=failed、error=already-installed`) while all three ledgers (manifest/node_modules/lock) had already been written to 0.10.1 — the host has a **post-write pseudo-failure** for its own bundle swap: installBundle writes through, the manifest then contains the name, and the host's completion path trips its own guard (self-referential artifact; the guard's `specPackageName` resolves package names from tarball URLs too). Separately: the 0.10.1 self-upgrade confirmation dialog broke the existing "click to upgrade" experience (owner ordered its removal).
- **Approach**: ① **reconcile on any D3 terminal failure** — re-read listInstalled: if the target version is already written, the upgrade factually happened and the result is re-judged as success (needsRestart=true prompting the activating restart, with the reconciliation noted in output); if the target is absent, the genuine-failure semantics stand; profileDir is threaded from host-api and both seams. ② **silent self-upgrade** — the confirmation dialog is removed wholesale (controlled state / modal / copy keys / SSR pin); the chip click upgrades directly and outcomes are still reported truthfully via banners. ADR-0022 decision 7 records the spike-inference correction, the reconciliation, and the UX ruling.
- **Verification**: typecheck clean; full suite (2 new pins T-D1 reconciled-success / T-D2 genuine-failure, silent structure pins replacing the confirmation pins; 18 pre-existing failures are the 028 machine-environment set, zero new); the real-machine event itself is the first dogfood datum: 0.10.1 landed via "restart to activate", guard journal anchors clean after restart.

### Fixes in 0.10.1 — three defects proven by same-day real-machine E2E of 0.10.0: enable-race retry universalized + account-reality re-read on rollback failure + guard hit always two-steps

- **Need (2026-10-17, proven during the same-day real-machine E2E of 0.10.0 — the skip-auth@0.4.1 upgrade event and the uninstall→reinstall event)**: ① the enable-stage activation race during plugin-tree reload windows (`pending (waiting for service: webRuntime)`) made the two-step upgrade's second leg, the rollback reinstall leg, and the D3 self-upgrade channel fail terminally — the 0.9.94-era "retry enable failures once (retry heals)" local enhancement covered only the first leg; ② a failed reinstall leg ≠ true absence — pnpm may have already written the package (activation failed afterwards), where advising "manually reinstall the old version" would mislead the user into a downgrade; ③ uninstall followed by a same-session reinstall: the host's live state / literal manifest still considers the package installed (guard fires) while the previousVersion probe reports "not installed", so 0.10.0's previousVersion precondition let the raw already-installed error surface.
- **Approach**: ① universalize the enable-race auto-retry — the first leg, the two-step second leg, the rollback reinstall leg, and the D3 channel all share the limited single retry (`installOnceRetryingEnable`; backoff and exempted failure shapes unchanged); ② re-read the ledger (listInstalled) before claiming absence — if written → "restart Desktop to finish activation" (with `details.installedVersion` surfaced truthfully), if truly absent → reinstall guidance; neither path suggests a misleading downgrade; ③ on the install path a guard hit always two-steps (remove+add is the official semantics; the previousVersion precondition is removed), and a failure without an old version to roll back to goes through the same ledger re-read. Incidental: the check-release gate now splits lines CRLF-tolerantly (fixing the bilingual top-entry false negative on CRLF working trees).
- **Verification**: typecheck clean; full suite 1595 tests (8 new pins: T-A1/A2/A3 enable-race on three legs + T-B1/B2 both ledger-re-read shapes + T-C1/C2/C3 three states of guard-hit two-step without an old version; 18 pre-existing failures are the 028 machine-environment set, zero new); real-machine recovery record: skip-auth 0.4.1 recovered via "restart to activate" (zero reinstall), guard journal anchors clean after restart.

### Feature + fix in 0.10.0 — host 0.2.1 already-installed adaptation: third-party two-step upgrade + rollback-reinstall degraded restore + dedicated self-upgrade channel (D3)

- **Need (2026-10-17, the owner)**: on the new DSH (0.2.1-rc.1/rc.2) dsh-m could no longer upgrade or self-upgrade any package. Root cause: the host's `installBundle()` gained an already-installed guard — when the package name resolved from a registry spec already exists in the profile manifest it is rejected before pnpm runs, and the official upgrade semantics became "uninstall first, then install"; tarball/git specs are exempt because their package name is unknowable before pnpm (verified on a real machine: installing a tarball URL over the installed dsh-m succeeded, activation shape restart-required). Scope: in-product desktop upgrade/self-upgrade fully broken; uninstall, toggle, fresh install, and the web path unaffected. Version 0.9.95+ skipped straight to 0.10.0.
- **Approach (ADR-0022, grilled decisions R1–R15)**: ① **install-first adaptive two-step** — no host version probing; only when the first leg returns `already-installed` does it convert to remove + retry (at most once, no loops); the install path silently two-steps on the guard too (installing an installed package is an upgrade), while fresh installs keep zero `removeBundle` calls; release-age/build-blocked/cancelled shapes take precedence over the guard check. ② **Rollback reinstall** — when the install leg terminates in failure the old version is reinstalled automatically: success yields the new **degraded restore** outcome (success shape + degraded/restoredTo; the client projects a warn that never enters the "upgraded vX→vY" arm, the operation pump reuses the existing `warned` state, the settings red dot keeps the existing failed||warned rule, and ✕ dismissal is unchanged); a failed reinstall is the hard `rollback-failed` absence error (with `details.pendingBuilds` surfaced truthfully when the reinstall leg itself is build-blocked); cancellation reports "restored the old version / no upgrade was made" and never masquerades as degraded; a build-blocked install leg still raises the approval request after the rollback has secured the old version. ③ **Channel separation** — third-party plugins use the official two-step; dsh-m's own self-upgrade uses the dedicated tarball direct-install channel (exact version → live-registry `dist.tarball` → intent pre-write → `installBundle(tarballUrl)` → in-place settle), rerouted at a single core seam (`SELF_PKG='dsh-m'`) with zero special-casing in host-api/agent tools/client, and never falling into remove+add; all entries preserved: the header chip gains a confirmation dialog (no uninstall involved, the current version stays intact on failure, a Desktop restart is needed afterwards), and `needsRestart` faithfully passes through the host's `application` (applied → no banner, restart-required → restart prompt). ④ **Boot reconciliation** — a self-upgrade intent ledger (`$DSH_HOME/dshm/self-upgrade-intent.json`; ADR-0018's panel-channel-zero-external-logging invariant intact) serves as a crash safety net: the normal path settles in-process, and only a process that died mid-upgrade is reconciled by the next self-check, once per process, against the actually installed version. ⑤ The mismatched self-upgrade WRITE_GUIDANCE copy no longer sends users "back to DSH Web", pointing to the official Settings → Plugins remove + install (exact version) instead. Seven GLOSSARY terms registered (two-step upgrade / already-installed guard / install-first adaptation / rollback reinstall / degraded restore / dedicated self-upgrade channel / boot reconciliation).
- **Verification**: typecheck clean; full suite 1585 tests (18 pre-existing environment-bound failures per know-how 028, zero new — the clean-tree comparison yields the same set: symlink-EPERM family + client-lightbox ESM scheme + CRLF source anchor + network fetch); new pins cover plan T1–T17 (intent-ledger storage five states / npmTarballUrl three pins / two-step-rollback-degraded boundary nine pins including remove-failed short-circuit, cancelled copy, and reinstall build-blocked pendingBuilds passthrough / D3 and the SELF_PKG seam rerouting five pins / host-api boot reconciliation plus the upgrade(dsh-m) rerouting end-to-end / client degraded projection, two-site wiring, confirmation-dialog ordering, and pump accounting); ADR-0022 filed, layered commits (feat core/client + docs adr/glossary + chore release).

### Refactor + fix in 0.9.94 — client-side mutation-outcome copy single-sourced (op-outcome) + patch-mapping YAML quote tolerance and duplicate-key elimination

- **Need (2026-10-17 architecture walk + nine grilled decisions)**: the presentation of mutation outcomes had no owning module — the guard-interception copy was assembled verbatim three times in main.jsx (0.9.93's toWire converged the wire format while the client-side presentation remained the known leftover; the multi-point hand-patching recurrence when `repairBasis` was added had already been demonstrated once), the upgrade-success copy twice verbatim, the builds suffix four times, superseded five times; separately: the transaction's compensation was blind to **non-double-quoted in-place forms** of workspace `patchedDependencies` (pnpm's single-quoted/unquoted spellings) via verbatim duplicate checks — the verbatim check could fabricate a duplicate-key file and the read-back check could false-pass (the same-package chain is unreachable in production because strip removes it first; this item is defensive-surface convergence and a fail-closed gap-fill).
- **Approach**: a new pure module `src/client/op-outcome.js` (GLOSSARY term "Mutation Outcome Copy" registered) — a factory `createOpOutcome(lookup)` producing finished copy (the `createMarkdown(h)` injection precedent) with four functions `guardBlockedText`/`buildsSuffix`/`upgradeOkText`/`mutationFailed`; six wiring sites (five projection families) become one-line calls, while flow-specific branches (compat dialog / capability-rejection banner / optimistic note) and the ToolCardRow short variant stay behind with an annotation. D9: the workspace leg of `verifyPatchMapping` now judges key membership via real yaml parsing (parse failure fails closed with "read-back failed"); `restorePatchMapping` parse-dedupes before appending (skip when the key exists in any quoting form; on parse failure it degrades to today's verbatim line check).
- **Verification**: full suite 1567 green (new client-op-outcome parametrized matrix of 32 cases — guardBlockedText/buildsSuffix/upgradeOkText to×activation 3×3 full cross/mutationFailed × five verbs, expected strings all hand-written literals; five transaction patch-mapping pins — single-quote dedupe / unquoted dedupe / simulated pnpm parse-explosion end-to-end / malformed-YAML false-committed → fail-closed / restore degradation does not throw, with the cross-package fixture premise pinned), typecheck clean; convergence anchors in main.jsx: `join(" | ")` 3→0, `mutationFailed` ≥ 6, and the `upgradeNotify` destructure removed along with its call sites; released after a four-round 15-finding plan review plus a hard-stopped two-round execution review (baseline independently re-run at 1530 for comparison + TDD red→green independently re-enacted); implementation plan in `docs/plans/2026-10-17-op-outcome-and-verify-quote-fix-implementation-plan.md`.

### Refactor in 0.9.93 — post-install guard wire format converged onto InstallGuardError.toWire; adding a guard field now touches 8 → 2 points (pure refactor, zero wire-format semantic change)

- **Need (2026-10-16 architecture walk, friction point 3, the walk's top pick)**: the `InstallGuardError` → wire-format projection was hand-written verbatim in 5 places (host-api 409 payload / tools install+upgrade arms / cli install+upgrade arms) plus one more assembly in the client — adding a guard field meant touching 8 points (class + 5 projections + client read + tests); the later addition of `repairBasis` had already demonstrated the recurrence once.
- **Approach**: a source method `toWire(): InstallGuardWire` (interface exported alongside the class, plus a `GuardViolation` re-export) becomes the single source of the shared 6-field wire format; the five projections become one-line spreads (envelope differences stay per-end: host-api `ok/error`, tools `ok/guard/message`, cli bare JSON), and the "repairBasis key absent when undefined" semantics move into the method body. Adding a field now touches 2 points (class+toWire, and the client read — the browser bundle cannot import core modules, so its defensive assembly stays, honestly noted). Deliberate delta: the tools envelope swaps key order between kind/message (JSON key order carries no consumer semantics; no raw-JSON key-order pins exist across tests/).
- **Verification**: full suite 1530 green (three new toWire pins: six fields / repairBasis key absence / key order; a new host-api dispatcher 409 pin closing the "host-api projection had zero test coverage" gap; tools-arm execute pins migrated zero), typecheck clean; the convergence anchor `repairBasis ? { repairBasis: … }` went 5 → 0 across the three files (also proving there is no 6th projection); plan review (two rounds, four findings) and execution review (two rounds, including A1 extracting the pin into its own describe) recorded in `docs/plans/2026-10-17-install-guard-towire-implementation-plan.md`.

### Fix + refactor in 0.9.92 — unified three-end market query intake (market-query single entry); CLI invalid-category silent swallow fixed

- **Need (2026-10-16, reproduced live)**: `dshm search --category <invalid>` silently printed "no matching entries" and exited 0, while the same input gets a 400 from the panel API and an explicit error from the agent tool — breaking the "same source, same semantics across GUI / tools / CLI" promise. Root cause: the category slug regex, source/offset/limit parsing, and the curated label map each had per-end copies (regex ×3, parsing ×3, labels ×2, deadline constants ×2), and the CLI copy lacked category validation.
- **Approach**: new module `src/core/market-query.ts` (GLOSSARY "Market Query Intake") is the single source of the shared grammar — one entry point `parseMarketQueryInput(raw, policy)` covers category (curated five ∪ community slug, validate-and-pass trimmed), source (three-value enum), limit (floor-first + per-end policy: GUI 96/32, tools/CLI 80/10), and offset; grammar violations throw `MarketQueryError` with byte-identical legacy messages, and each end maps to its own envelope (HTTP 400 / thrown tool error / CLI dispatch catch `错误：` + exit 1). Also single-sourced: the slug regex (including the catalog adapter's third copy), the curated label map (keyed off registry `CATEGORIES` with a drift-guard test), and the deadline constants; the CLI help category list now shows the current curated five (the old list was a pre-0.9.16 leftover). Deliberate deltas: (1) tools' in-range fractional limits were implicitly floored by core `clampLimit`; the floor now happens explicitly at intake (same values pointwise; output objects carry no limit field); (2) CLI category/source are now validated after trimming, matching host-api/tools (`' self-dev '` used to match nothing and `--source ' all '` used to error); (3) dual-violation requests now report category before source (single-violation messages byte-identical).
